# Seed 80 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - 12 hours: nothing gained - the fourth run with the freezes lifted, slow at equal effort as predicted (Iteration 566)

`seed 80 12h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16` at 605b18a61. The chain: done. 45,696 designs, generations 0-178: 26,610 ok, 11,794 stopped after one round, 6,934 screened, 307 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 42 wrong output: apps, 9 died: kernel workload; 3,808 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 258 designs - the report's 258, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 258 (258 inside the box), hypervolume 0.33644 | fastest 0.1470, smallest 13989, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1773. **The stopping rule** (stopping.py, results/stopping-series.md): verdict: seed 80 (at its last generation, 178 - the earlier runs there too) gains nothing - the hypervolume 0.33644 against 0.35169 (-4.3%); the fastest 0.1439 against 0.1378 (+4.5%); the smallest 13,989 against 14,015 (-0.2%); the least memory 112 KB against 108 KB (+4 KB); the fastest at 32 bits 0.1758 against 0.1643 (+7.0%) the count: 4 run(s) on the method; the earliest stop after seed 81, if every run until then gains nothing

**The extremes**: fastest 15edf22e11 0.1470, smallest 404034e887 13,989 bytes, least memory 112 KB, fastest at 32 bits 5d41099642 0.1773.

**One round first**: 11,794 children stopped after one round, 30.5% of those measured; 1224 audited in full, 0 of them on the front.

**The operators**: borrowed 40.6%, crossover 26.3%, mutation 33.1% of the children; the last fifth borrowed 43.6%, crossover 23.2%, mutation 33.2%; the front's entries per child 0.566% (258 of 45,562).

**The JIT designs**: 78 of the front, 25 of its fastest tenth's 25; jitmul 49, jitfuse 12, jitexec 3, jitos 22, jitrp 11.

**The fast end re-measured** (remeasure.json, 112 designs): the fastest ten 1.068 re-measured / in-run, the JIT designs 1.039, the rest 1.002.

**The experiments**: the spread at generation 126 - | build/evolve/db.jsonl | 126 | 18896 | 369 | 0.1444 | 14,089 | 112 | 1.0055e+06 | 0.6120 | 0.1825 |; appbench - **Spearman, in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean: 0.932 over 16 designs.**; the clock test - none; truefronts - none.

**The remote checks** (results/xcheck-seed80.md): dream.lan-prebuilt 24 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.877; dream.lan-prebuilt32 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.911; dream.lan-prebuilt64 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.939; rage.lan-native 16 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.082.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve (held out): 258 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.952 at the top, where selection chooses: the fastest third (86) 0.821, the fastest tenth (25) 0.755 regret: the selection's fastest, 15edf22e11, 0.076 held out; the held-out fastest, a9bfd3745f, 0.066 - the pick +15.2%

**The curse** (curse.txt - rule 3, 0.98-1.05 works): fastest tenth    8 designs: median 1.070, range 1.041-1.102 fastest third   28 designs: median 1.041, range 0.980-1.102

**Progress**: the smallest binary + image (exact) gained in 27 generations; the last at generation 154 of 178; in the last third 5 gains, 100 bytes in all the best speed gained 2% or more in 17 generations; the last at generation 69 (31: 3.7%, 34: 3.8%, 36: 3.9%, 37: 5.8%, 69: 6.1%)

**The votes**: all four this run: scale, sharedcall, msc, rtimm, rtempty, noinls, opcall, jitdoes, jitrp, jitos, alignhot, lto, gotodup, regpin over the last 3 runs in the ledger (78, 79, 80): candidates - scale (12 of 12), sharedcall (12 of 12), msc (12 of 12), jitdoes (12 of 12), jitrp (12 of 12), jitos (12 of 12), alignhot (12 of 12); no keep vote and half the votes or more - none

**The rules** (rules.txt): 10 rules; 1 (The verdict) SEE, 3 (The fast gate) ATTENTION, 4 (The freeze) ATTENTION, 8 (tail) ATTENTION, 9 (The rtimm=2 / rtfuse overlap) PROPOSE, 10 (Programs past 4 MB) SEE, the rest PASS

**The freezes lifted** (lifted.py): prediction 556 (the freezes were right; values as built): HOLDS - bytehdr, thinhdr, bss, lean off 1 on 5%- : yes; alignhot, msc, sharedcall each on under 10%: yes; doesfar and varcall off 1 nowhere: yes

**At 32 bits** (fail32.txt): at 32 bits: 27888 designs measured, 53 failed expected, no fault: 53 of 53 (cpt16's reach at 4-byte cells - Iteration 517); not a known cause: 0


**The deaths**: 42 wrong output: apps, 307 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 9 died: kernel workload. deaths-check.py rebuilt them here (design, generation, in the run, genes, here): adc77c3487 - 45 - wrong output: apps - jit - DIFFERS from s6; 2d4a69fc8d - 125 - wrong output: apps - jit+tracer - DIFFERS from s6.

**The chain on master**: process-checks.sh at 3d7ebe2: build 0, run-tests PASS, validate 7 of 7 IDENTICAL, the gate alive 284 of 284, ans-check 0 of 68 differ (its negative control caught), deaths-check: 0 of 2 rebuilt compute right here; 2 open - OPEN, a correctness issue: seed 80: jitexec with alignhot=3 segfaults on sim16's nested EXECUTE (2 designs); alignhot built where express() drops it - results/drafts/jitexec-alignhot-566.md, the jail PASS.

**The verdict** (rule 1): no comparison step - the spread at generation 126 (session 67): seed 80 inside the lifted runs' band - the fastest 0.1444 (seeds 77-79: 0.1418-0.1477; seed 76 0.1383), the smallest 14,089 (the second smallest of five), 112 KB, the spread's hypervolume 0.6120 (only seed 79's lower). The four runs with the freezes lifted (77-80: 0.1418, 0.1477, 0.1435, 0.1444 at generation 126) are all slower at the fast end than the three with them on (74-76: 0.1381-0.1389) - 4 against 3 wholly apart, one-sided p = 1/35 = 0.029.

**The stopping rule** (matched by generation): **seed 80 gains nothing** - at its last generation, 178, against seed 79 there (177, its end): the hypervolume 0.33644 against 0.35169 (-4.3%), the fastest 0.1439 against 0.1378 (+4.5%), the smallest 13,989 against 14,015 (-0.2%, the bar 1%), the memory 112 KB against 108, the fastest at 32 bits 0.1758 against 0.1643 (+7.0%). Its best speed made its last 2% gain at generation 69. The count: four runs on the method, the last gaining nothing - the earliest stop after seed 81, if it gains nothing.

**Prediction 565** (seed 80 slow at generation 126, nothing gained at its end): **holds** - 0.1444 at 126, above 0.1389; nothing gained at 178. **Prediction 556** (the freezes were right; values as built): **holds** (lifted.py).

**The remote checks**: dream agrees (0.877, 32-bit 0.911, 64-bit 0.939). **The Tegra's disagreement explained - the sample, not the host** (seed 79's question): rage builds designs itself, so only GCC's without the JIT, and the compiler gene has gone to clang - the true fronts' designs rage can build: seeds 74-80 4, 15, 22, 113, 19, 25, 0. The agreement follows the speed span of what it measured: the checks of seeds 75-77's fronts (spans x2.2, x1.8, x2.8) gave 0.832, 0.921, 0.926; of seeds 78-79's (x1.26, x1.25 - all at the fast end, 0.30-0.39) -0.103 and 0.082 - over so narrow a band the rank is noise (here / laptop 0.87-1.16). Seed 80's front holds none: seed 81's check would have nothing to sample - REMOTES' --also (566) asks rage for 16 of seed 77's front by id instead (0.34-0.96, five of them measured there in seed 78's chain), a direct test of the host.

**run-tests on master** (566): the generators test failed once this file was in the tree - engines-check.py takes each list gene's value from the newest recorded design, and seed 80's newest opcall, ['PLACE'], is offered only where PLACE is a format-10 word: dropped on s6, the variant NOT EXPRESSED. The donor now takes the newest value the variant expresses (['ROT']); the checks below ran after the fix.

**The open deaths** (deaths-check: 2 of 2 rebuilt still wrong): adc77c3487 and 2d4a69fc8d, JIT designs, wrong on apps - each stops at sim16's RUN; the minimal design s6 + tag2, jit, jitx, jitexec, alignhot=3 segfaults there. alignhot was frozen until seed 77; and both designs build it though express() drops it (align1, alignfn) - an open correctness issue, results/drafts/jitexec-alignhot-566.md.

**The experiments**: appbench's Spearman 0.932 over 16 (seed 79 0.806); held out, the fastest tenth 0.755 (seed 79 0.551), the pick's regret +15.2%.

# Evolved VM designs

38762 designs evaluated, 26610 alive, 6934 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 712b2a719d, speed 0.365, binary + image 19,900, memory 140 KB - Q 0.301 of s6-cv8b's.
